Task ID: SCORE-4
Agent: Security Scoring Auditor (read-only)
Task: Score 10 security categories (1-10) for the Vite + React + Supabase LottaCash casino. No code changes — audit-only. Score the CODE as written; dormant vulns still count.

Work Log:
- Read worklog context (Task 0, A, B, C, D, SCORE-1, AUDIT-2, SCORE-2, SCORE-3) for prior findings — especially SCORE-1/AUDIT-2 (RTP/local-play math) and SCORE-3 (cb_claim_payout idempotency hole + dead V1 code).
- Read auth/session/guest layer: `src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx`, `src/lib/supabase.ts`, `src/components/AdminRoute/AdminRoute.tsx`, `src/components/ProtectedRoute/ProtectedRoute.tsx`, `src/lib/authRedirect.ts`, `src/lib/signupVerification.ts`, `supabase/functions/{send-signup-code,verify-signup-code,send-password-reset-code,reset-password-with-code}/index.ts`, `supabase/config.toml`.
- Read wallet & game-bet edge functions: `supabase/functions/{place-limbo-bet,place-crash-bet,cash-out-crash,place-roulette-bet,place-slots-bet,place-keno-bet,mines-game,blackjack-game,get-deposit-address,poll-deposits,sweep-deposits,case-battle-v2}/index.ts` + `_shared/{cors,config,crypto-wallet,sweep,rtp,rtpBias}.ts`.
- Read all 8 local-play fallback resolvers: `src/lib/local-play.ts` (345 lines), `src/lib/local-case-battles.ts` (234 lines).
- Read the full SQL schema: `supabase/lottacash-complete-setup.sql` (4,782 lines) and `supabase/case-battles-v2-setup.sql` (309 lines) — focused on RLS policies, column-level grants, security-definer RPCs, profiles_guard_balance / profiles_guard_admin triggers, admin RPCs, self-exclusion, deposit-limit, and provably-fair seed rotation.
- Read admin layer: `src/lib/admin.ts` (436 lines), `src/pages/Admin/Admin.tsx`, `src/components/AdminRoute/AdminRoute.tsx`.
- Read compliance / RG copy: `src/pages/ResponsibleGaming/ResponsibleGaming.tsx`, `src/pages/Signup/Signup.tsx`, `src/pages/Withdraw/Withdraw.tsx`, `src/pages/SweepstakesRules/SweepstakesRules.tsx`, `src/content/legal.ts`, `src/lib/responsibleGaming.ts`.
- Read headers/CORS config: `vercel.json`, `static-server.mjs`, `vite.config.ts`, `index.html`, `supabase/functions/_shared/cors.ts`.
- Read secrets handling: `.gitignore`, `supabase/functions/_shared/{config,crypto-wallet,health}.ts`, `scripts/sweep.env.example`.
- Verified absence of CSP/X-Frame-Options/X-Content-Type-Options/HSTS/Referrer-Policy anywhere in source via grep.

Key CRITICAL findings (file:line evidence):

1. **`profiles.sweeps_coins` is user-writable** — `lottacash-complete-setup.sql:714` policy "Users can update own profile" (`using (auth.uid() = id)`, no `with check`, no column restriction) + trigger `profiles_prevent_balance_change` (line 1074) ONLY reverts `balance`, NOT `sweeps_coins`. Any authenticated user can run `supabase.from('profiles').update({sweeps_coins: 9999999}).eq('id', myId)` and the update sticks. SC has $0.01 redemption value (100 SC = $1) — direct money exploit via `request_sc_redemption` (`lottacash-complete-setup.sql:1766`). Same hole exposes `total_wagered`, `total_wins`, `total_losses`, `daily_deposit_limit`, `weekly_deposit_limit`, `self_excluded_until`.

2. **`crash_bets.crash_point` readable by user before round resolves** — `lottacash-complete-setup.sql:956` `grant select on public.crash_bets to authenticated;` (table-level, NOT column-restricted) + RLS policy line 882 "Users read own crash bets" (`auth.uid() = user_id`). The `place-crash-bet` edge function deliberately does NOT return `crashPoint` in its response (`supabase/functions/place-crash-bet/index.ts:156-170` with explicit "do NOT return crashPoint" comment), but the user can immediately run `supabase.from('crash_bets').select('crash_point').eq('id', justPlacedBetId).single()` and read the bust multiplier for their pending bet — then cash out at `crashPoint - 0.01` for a guaranteed win. The `cash_out_crash` SQL (line 4507) validates `p_cashed_at <= b.crash_point` but doesn't help if the user already knows `crash_point`. CRITICAL provably-fair violation. (Compare: `game_pf_seeds`, `mines_games`, `blackjack_hands` correctly use column-level grants at lines 943-950 to exclude `server_seed`/`mine_tiles`/`shoe`.)

3. **`case_battles.internal_seed` publicly readable during `committing`/`running` phases** — `case-battles-v2-setup.sql:91` policy "Anyone can read case battles" (`using (true)`) + line 98 `grant select on public.case_battles to authenticated;` (table-level). The `internal_seed` column (line 39 "generated on start, revealed on completion") is the LIVE secret seed — but it's readable by ANY authenticated user (not just participants) the moment `handleStart` writes it (`supabase/functions/case-battle-v2/index.ts:703-709`). With `internal_seed` + `eos_block_target`, an attacker can poll the EOS RPC, derive `battle_seed = SHA-256(internal_seed:eos_block_id)` before the edge function resolves, and pre-compute every player's drops. The `seed_hash` commitment (which IS meant to be public) is undermined because the underlying seed is also exposed. CRITICAL provably-fair violation.

4. **`cb_claim_payout` accepts client-supplied `p_amount` with no idempotency** — `case-battles-v2-setup.sql:271-308`: `cb_claim_payout(p_battle_id uuid, p_slot int, p_amount numeric)` grants `execute to authenticated`. The RPC checks the caller owns the slot but **does NOT verify `p_amount` matches actual battle winnings** (no recompute from `case_battle_drops`) and **does NOT track "already claimed" state**. Any winner can call this RPC directly via `supabase.rpc('cb_claim_payout', {p_battle_id, p_slot, p_amount: 9999999})` to credit arbitrary GC balance, OR call it twice to double-credit. The V2 edge function `handleClaim` (`case-battle-v2/index.ts:970-1068`) recomputes `p_amount` server-side from drops, but the RPC is also exposed directly to authenticated users — bypassing the edge function. The edge function comment (lines 965-968) explicitly admits: "the v2 `cb_claim_payout` SQL doesn't track 'already claimed' state, so a duplicate claim would double-credit. The frontend must guard against this." Frontend-guarded money endpoints are unsafe.

5. **`cb_create_battle` / `cb_join_battle` don't debit entry cost** — `case-battles-v2-setup.sql:145-159` inserts a battle row with `pot_total = p_entry_cost` but NEVER calls any debit function. Same for `cb_join_battle` (line 201: `pot_total = pot_total + v_battle.entry_cost` — pot grows but no user balance is debited). The V2 edge function `handleStart` (`case-battle-v2/index.ts:630-631`) comment says "debits are expected to have happened in `cb_create_battle` / `cb_join_battle`" — but they didn't. Result: any authenticated user can create/join case battles for free, win real drops, and claim them via `cb_claim_payout`. Combined with finding #4, this is a free-money fountain (GC only, since `cb_claim_payout` hardcodes `balance` column at line 299).

6. **`cancel_self_exclusion()` lets users cancel their own exclusion immediately** — `lottacash-complete-setup.sql:2056-2074`: `cancel_self_exclusion()` is `grant execute to authenticated` and unconditionally sets `self_excluded_until = null` for `auth.uid()`. The `ResponsibleGaming.tsx` page (line 59) explicitly promises: "The exclusion cannot be lifted early." The code directly contradicts the user-facing copy and responsible-gaming norms. A user can self-exclude → immediately cancel → resume betting within seconds.

7. **Deposit limits are stored but NEVER enforced** — `set_deposit_limits` (line 2130) writes `daily_deposit_limit`/`weekly_deposit_limit` to `profiles`. `credit_crypto_deposit` (line 1278) credits the deposit WITHOUT checking either limit. The `ResponsibleGaming.tsx` page (line 49) claims: "Once a limit is set, deposits that would exceed it are blocked at the chain-scan layer." This is false — the `poll-deposits` cron and `credit_crypto_deposit` RPC have no limit check. User-facing copy lies about a safety feature that doesn't exist.

8. **No server-side age verification** — `Signup.tsx:96-106` does a client-side age check (>= 18) from `birthDate`. The `birth_date` is sent to `send-signup-code` and `verify-signup-code` edge functions (`src/lib/signupVerification.ts:5,24`) but **both edge functions completely ignore `birth_date`** — it's never validated, never stored. `verify-signup-code/index.ts` doesn't even parse `body.birth_date`. A user can bypass age verification by calling the edge function directly (no `birth_date` required). The Privacy Policy claims "date of birth (for age verification)" but the data is never collected server-side.

9. **No geo-blocking** — Sweepstakes Rules (`content/legal.ts` line 54) say "void in jurisdictions where prohibited by law, including but not limited to Washington, Idaho." No IP-based geo check exists at signup, bet, or redemption time. Users in restricted jurisdictions can sign up and play freely.

10. **No security headers anywhere** — `vercel.json` only sets `Cache-Control` on `/assets/(.*)`. No CSP, no X-Frame-Options, no X-Content-Type-Options, no Strict-Transport-Security, no Referrer-Policy in `vercel.json`, `static-server.mjs`, `vite.config.ts`, or `index.html` (no `<meta http-equiv>` tags). Site is iframe-clickjackable and XSS-defense-in-depth is absent.

11. **CORS open by default** — `supabase/functions/_shared/cors.ts:10-19` `getAllowedOrigin` falls back to `*` when `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` is unset, with only a `console.warn`. A production deployment that forgets to set `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` leaves every Edge Function open to cross-origin requests from any domain. The `Access-Control-Allow-Headers` also exposes `x-cron-secret` (minor info leak).

12. **`crash_settle_loss(p_bet_id)` has no user_id check** — `lottacash-complete-setup.sql:4538-4561`: the function `select * into b from public.crash_bets where id = p_bet_id for update;` doesn't filter by `user_id`. It's `grant execute ... to service_role` only, so direct user calls are blocked — but if any edge function ever lets a user pass `p_bet_id` of another user's bet, the function would mark that bet as a loss for the other user. Currently not exploitable but fragile.

13. **`AdminRoute` is client-side only** — `src/components/AdminRoute/AdminRoute.tsx:21-23` comment correctly notes: "This is a client-side convenience check only. Real authorization MUST be enforced server-side via Supabase RLS policies and `is_current_user_admin` on every admin RPC — client checks can be bypassed by anyone who opens DevTools." Server-side checks ARE in place (every admin RPC calls `require_admin()` or inline `is_admin` lookup) — so this is correctly designed. Audit trail gap: only `admin_credit_user` writes to `admin_credit_log` (line 1757). `admin_set_user_admin`, `admin_complete_crypto_withdrawal`, `admin_fail_crypto_withdrawal`, `admin_process_redemption` do NOT write to a unified audit log (redemptions sets `processed_by` on the row, but no per-action audit trail).

14. **V1 `case-battle` edge function has `verify_jwt = false`** — `supabase/config.toml:27-28`: `[functions.case-battle] verify_jwt = false`. The V1 edge function (892 lines, dead per SCORE-3) is deployed without JWT verification. Anyone can invoke it without auth. Dead-code risk: if the function still has any exploitable path (e.g. reads `body.user_id` and credits balance), it's open to the internet. Recommended: delete V1 function or set `verify_jwt = true`.

15. **Local-play wallet is trivially inflatable** — `src/lib/local-play.ts:14-27` stores `lottacash:local:balance` and `lottacash:local:sweeps` in `localStorage` as plaintext numbers. A user can `localStorage.setItem('lottacash:local:sweeps', '999999')` to give themselves unlimited SC in local-play mode. `localDebit` (line 32-37) also has a TOCTOU race between `localBalance()` read and `writeNum()` write — multi-tab could double-spend the local wallet. Low severity since local-play is demo-only (no real backend), but if the fallback ever runs in production for a real user, the wallet is unprotected.

16. **`AUDIT_BYPASS` build flag synthesizes fake admin user** — `src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx:50-74`: when `VITE_AUDIT_BYPASS === "1"`, the auth provider synthesizes a fake `User` with `role: "authenticated"` and a fake `Session` with hardcoded `access_token: "audit-fake-access-token"`. This is a build-time flag (Vite inlines `import.meta.env`), so it can't be flipped at runtime — but if a production build is accidentally created with `VITE_AUDIT_BYPASS=1` in the build env, every visitor is treated as a logged-in user with a fake session. Mitigation: the flag only enables UI navigation; real backend RPCs still require a valid Supabase JWT. So it's a UI-spoofing risk, not a backend-bypass risk.

17. **`chat_messages` PUBLIC-readable to authenticated** — `lottacash-complete-setup.sql:794-798`: policy "Authenticated users read chat" with `using (true)`. Any logged-in user sees the ENTIRE chat history of every user. PII risk if users share personal info. No rate limiting on chat inserts (`policy "Users post own chat messages"` allows insert with `auth.uid() = user_id`, no throttle).

18. **`request_sc_redemption` doesn't check self-exclusion** — `lottacash-complete-setup.sql:1766-1828`: debits SC and creates a redemption row but never calls `check_user_self_exclusion`. A self-excluded user can still redeem SC for cash. Self-exclusion is only enforced on bet placement (via edge functions), not on withdrawals/redemptions.

Stage Summary:
- **3 CRITICAL money exploits** (sweeps_coins direct update, cb_claim_payout arbitrary amount + double-claim, free case battles via missing entry-cost debit).
- **2 CRITICAL provably-fair violations** (crash_bets.crash_point readable pre-resolution, case_battles.internal_seed readable during committing/running).
- **2 CRITICAL responsible-gaming lies** (cancel_self_exclusion lets users lift exclusion immediately; deposit limits stored but never enforced despite page copy claiming enforcement).
- **2 CRITICAL sweepstakes-compliance gaps** (no server-side age verification; no geo-blocking).
- **2 HIGH security-header/CORS gaps** (no CSP/X-Frame/HSTS/X-Content-Type/Referrer-Policy; CORS open by default).
- **1 HIGH audit-trail gap** (only `admin_credit_user` is logged; admin role grants, withdrawal completions, redemption processing have no unified audit log).
- **3 MEDIUM issues** (V1 edge function `verify_jwt=false`, request_sc_redemption skips self-exclusion check, chat PII exposure with no rate limit).

No file changes were made — audit-only.

Final SCORE-4 scores (/10):

| Category | Score | Justification |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Authentication security | 6 | Supabase sessions use standard `persistSession+autoRefreshToken` (`src/lib/supabase.ts:41-44`); signup/password-reset codes are hashed with 5-attempt cap + 10-min TTL (`verify-signup-code/index.ts:5,64`); no MFA. Guest mode synthesizes a fake `User` with `role:"authenticated"` (`AuthContext.tsx:86-94`) and `VITE_AUDIT_BYPASS` synthesizes a fake admin session (`AuthContext.tsx:50-74`) — build-time flag, UI-only spoofing risk. Tokens in localStorage (XSS-vulnerable but Supabase default). |
| 2. Wallet security | 3 | Server-side `for update` row locks in `place_crash_bet`/`cash_out_crash`/`settle_limbo_bet` prevent race conditions (`lottacash-complete-setup.sql:4445,4498`). BUT `cb_claim_payout` (`case-battles-v2-setup.sql:271`) accepts client-supplied `p_amount` with no idempotency — double-claim and arbitrary-amount exploit. `cb_create_battle`/`cb_join_battle` don't debit entry cost (line 145, 201). Local-play wallet (`local-play.ts:14-27`) is plaintext localStorage with TOCTOU race in `localDebit` (line 32-37). |
| 3. RTP tamper-resistance | 3 | Game outcomes use HMAC(server_seed, clientSeed:nonce) server-side (`place-limbo-bet/index.ts:29-37`, `_shared/limbo.ts`); server seed never revealed until `rotate_server_seed()` (`lottacash-complete-setup.sql:2723`). BUT `crash_bets.crash_point` is readable pre-resolution via table-level `grant select` (line 956) + RLS — defeats Crash provably-fair commitment. `case_battles.internal_seed` publicly readable during committing/running (`case-battles-v2-setup.sql:91,98`). Mines `force_mine` bias uses a second HMAC the player can't pre-verify against the committed mine_tiles. |
| 4. Client-side trust | 7 | All real-money outcomes computed server-side in edge functions using server seeds; client `provablyFair.ts` modules are verification-only (`VerifyRoundTool.tsx`). Crash `cashedAtMultiplier` validated server-side against `b.crash_point` (`lottacash-complete-setup.sql:4507`). Ding: client can read `crash_point` directly from DB (see #3), undermining the otherwise-correct server-side validation. `settle_slots_bet`/`settle_roulette_bet` trust server-computed reels/pocket without SQL re-validation (acceptable since edge function is server-side). |
| 5. Secrets handling | 8 | All secrets via `Deno.env.get(...)` in edge functions; `CRYPTO_MASTER_MNEMONIC` loaded server-side only (`_shared/crypto-wallet.ts:29-35`), private keys derived server-side and never returned to client. `CRON_SECRET` properly validated in `assertCronAuth` (`_shared/config.ts:35-46`). `.gitignore` excludes `.env`/`.env.local`/`scripts/sweep.env`. No hard-coded secrets. Minor: anon JWT in `.env` is public-by-design (Supabase); `VITE_AUDIT_BYPASS` is a build-time UI flag with no backend bypass. |
| 6. CORS & headers | 3 | `_shared/cors.ts:10-19` falls back to `*` if `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` unset (only `console.warn`) — production misconfiguration leaves API open. NO CSP, X-Frame-Options, X-Content-Type-Options, Strict-Transport-Security, or Referrer-Policy anywhere (`vercel.json`, `static-server.mjs`, `index.html` all lack them). Site is iframe-clickjackable. `Access-Control-Allow-Headers` exposes `x-cron-secret` header name (minor info leak). |
| 7. Sweepstakes compliance | 3 | GC/SC separation correct (`balance` vs `sweeps_coins` columns; `request_sc_redemption` debits SC). AMOE documented in `content/legal.ts:57` (PO Box, 14-day SLA) but no admin tool to credit mail-in SC. Age verification is client-side only (`Signup.tsx:96-106`); `verify-signup-code/index.ts` ignores `birth_date` entirely — bypassable by direct edge-function call. NO geo-blocking (no IP check at signup/bet/redemption) despite Sweepstakes Rules voiding WA/ID. NO KYC despite §7 "right to verify eligibility." No operator entity/license #/jurisdiction disclosed (per SCORE-2). |
| 8. Responsible gaming | 3 | Self-exclusion IS enforced at every bet edge function via `check_user_self_exclusion` RPC (`place-limbo-bet/index.ts:61-66` etc.). BUT `cancel_self_exclusion()` (`lottacash-complete-setup.sql:2056`) lets any user cancel their own exclusion immediately — contradicts `ResponsibleGaming.tsx:59` "cannot be lifted early." `set_deposit_limits` (line 2130) stores limits but `credit_crypto_deposit` (line 1278) NEVER checks them — page copy (`ResponsibleGaming.tsx:49`) falsely claims "blocked at the chain-scan layer." `request_sc_redemption` doesn't check self-exclusion. Session reminders exist (`useSessionReminder.ts`). |
| 9. Admin panel security | 6 | Server-side `require_admin()` / `is_admin` check in EVERY admin RPC (`lottacash-complete-setup.sql:1702,1734,1844`). `admin_set_user_admin` blocks self-modification (line 1704-1706). `AdminRoute.tsx` explicitly documents itself as client-side convenience only (line 21-23). BUT audit trail is incomplete: only `admin_credit_user` writes to `admin_credit_log` (line 1757); `admin_set_user_admin`, `admin_complete_crypto_withdrawal`, `admin_process_redemption` have no unified audit log (redemptions sets `processed_by` on row only). No 2FA for admin accounts. No IP allowlist. |
| 10. Data exposure | 3 | RLS enabled on every sensitive table (lines 680-701); column-level grants correctly exclude `server_seed`/`mine_tiles`/`shoe` (lines 943-950). BUT `sweeps_coins` is user-writable: `profiles_prevent_balance_change` trigger (line 1074) ONLY reverts `balance`, not `sweeps_coins` — any user can `update({sweeps_coins: 9999999})` their own profile and it sticks (SC has $0.01 redemption value = direct money exploit). `case_battles` + `case_battle_players` + `case_battle_drops` are PUBLIC-readable (`case-battles-v2-setup.sql:91,93,94`) — leaks `internal_seed` (see #3), player roster, and live drops. `chat_messages` PUBLIC-readable to authenticated (line 794-798) with no rate limit. `crash_bets` table-level grant leaks `crash_point` (see #3). |

Top-priority fixes (recommended for next pass):
1. **Fix `sweeps_coins` direct-update vulnerability** — extend `profiles_prevent_balance_change` trigger to also revert `sweeps_coins`, `total_wagered`, `total_wins`, `total_losses`, `total_deposited`, `total_withdrawn`, `daily_deposit_limit`, `weekly_deposit_limit`, and `self_excluded_until`. OR add column-level RLS / `with check` clause to the "Users can update own profile" policy restricting to a safe allowlist (username, avatar_seed, etc.).
2. **Add column-level grant on `crash_bets`** — `grant select (id, user_id, wager, won, payout, cashed_at, coin_type, nonce, created_at, completed_at) on public.crash_bets to authenticated;` — exclude `crash_point` from authenticated reads. Same pattern as `game_pf_seeds` / `mines_games` / `blackjack_hands`.
3. **Add column-level grant on `case_battles`** — exclude `internal_seed` (and possibly `battle_seed` until completion) from authenticated reads. Only `seed_hash` should be public pre-resolution.
4. **Fix `cb_claim_payout`** — recompute `p_amount` server-side inside the RPC from `case_battle_drops` (don't accept it as a parameter), and add a `claimed_at timestamptz` column or `case_battle_claims` table to enforce idempotency via `for update` + check-and-set.
5. **Add entry-cost debit to `cb_create_battle` / `cb_join_battle`** — call `place_limbo_bet`-style balance check + debit (or a new `debit_case_battle_entry` RPC) inside the create/join RPCs. Reject if insufficient balance.
6. **Remove `cancel_self_exclusion()` or restrict it to post-expiry** — either drop the function or only allow cancellation when `self_excluded_until < now()` (no-op). Update `ResponsibleGaming.tsx` copy if a cooling-off lift is intentionally offered.
7. **Enforce deposit limits in `credit_crypto_deposit`** — sum `crypto_deposits.usd_amount` for the user over the last 24h / 7d, compare to `daily_deposit_limit` / `weekly_deposit_limit`, raise exception if exceeded. Update `ResponsibleGaming.tsx` copy if the limit is advisory-only.
8. **Add server-side age verification** — in `verify-signup-code/index.ts`, parse `body.birth_date`, validate age >= 18, store in `profiles.birth_date` column (add column if missing). Reject signup if under 18.
9. **Add IP-based geo-blocking** — at minimum, check `req.headers.get('CF-IPCountry')` or similar in edge functions and block requests from restricted jurisdictions (WA, ID, etc.) at signup, bet, and redemption time.
10. **Add security headers to `vercel.json`** — CSP (with nonce or hash-based script-src), X-Frame-Options: DENY (or frame-ancestors in CSP), X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff, Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains; preload, Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin.
11. **Set `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` in production** — and change the `getAllowedOrigin` fallback from `*` to a hard reject (return 403 if Origin not allowed and ALLOWED_ORIGINS unset in production).
12. **Add unified admin audit log** — `admin_audit_log(admin_id, action, target_user_id, amount, metadata, created_at)` written by every admin RPC.
13. **Check self-exclusion in `request_sc_redemption`** — block redemption for self-excluded users.
14. **Delete V1 `case-battle` edge function** or set `verify_jwt = true` in `supabase/config.toml:27-28`.
15. **Rate-limit chat_messages inserts** — add a per-user throttle (e.g. 5 messages/minute) via a SQL trigger or edge-function check.
